| Root | 1112264 | jspdf | <=3.0.4 | >=4.0.0 | `. > jspdf@3.0.3` |

## Root: jspdf (1112264)

**Title:** jsPDF has Local File Inclusion/Path Traversal vulnerability

**Recommendation:** Upgrade to version 4.0.0 or later

**Patched versions:** >=4.0.0

**CVE(s):** CVE-2025-68428

**Advisory:** https://github.com/advisories/GHSA-f8cm-6447-x5h2

**Dependency paths:**

- `. > jspdf@3.0.3`

## Root: fast-xml-parser (1113568)

**Title:** fast-xml-parser has an entity encoding bypass via regex injection in DOCTYPE entity names

**Recommendation:** Upgrade to version 5.3.5 or later

**Patched versions:** >=5.3.5

**CVE(s):** CVE-2026-25896

**Advisory:** https://github.com/advisories/GHSA-m7jm-9gc2-mpf2

**Dependency paths:**

## Mobile: fast-xml-parser (1113567)

**Title:** fast-xml-parser has an entity encoding bypass via regex injection in DOCTYPE entity names

**Recommendation:** Upgrade to version 4.5.4 or later

**Patched versions:** >=4.5.4

**CVE(s):** CVE-2026-25896

**Advisory:** https://github.com/advisories/GHSA-m7jm-9gc2-mpf2

## Mobile: shell-quote (1120422)

**Title:** shell-quote quote() does not escape newlines in object .op values

**Recommendation:** Upgrade to version 1.8.4 or later

**Patched versions:** >=1.8.4

**CVE(s):** CVE-2026-9277

**Advisory:** https://github.com/advisories/GHSA-w7jw-789q-3m8p
